### 279 x1
Engineered rocket fitted with a special ^#FFDD00;proximity fuze^reset; allowing it to detonate over valid hostile targets, showering enemies in a hail of shrapnel. Unsurprisingly, unlike its HEAT counterpart it ^#FFDD00;cannot effectively engage armored vehicles.^reset; This type of munition finds effectiveness in open warfare scenarios which allow for a much more predictable flight path for indirect engagements of enemies. ^#FFDD00;Only compatible with the RPG-7 Launcher.^reset;

### 281 x1

### 282 x1
^#8FAED3;Charges 60 shots. Attuned staff would be somewhat comparable to the performance of a ballistic assault rifle.^reset;
Unlike spellcards of old, these attunement cards carefully prepared by the individual for their personal magic staff beforehand would allow them to ready their charges before a fight. In the heat of battle, one could reach for their spellcard as if it were ammunition and focus on it, recharging their magical potential far faster than if one were to have brought a traditional sorcerer's book.
### 283 x1
Spellcard 'Assault' Type
### 284 x1
^#8FAED3;Charges 16 shots. Attuned staff would be somewhat comparable to the performance of an automatic shotgun.^reset;
An attunement first pioneered during the early reign of the Dynasty. This spellcard focuses an extreme amount of kinetic energy in a few telekinetically-held projectiles (lead pellets are preferred, but gravel and sand can work). When desired, the projectiles are launched at immense speed in a cone of devastating shrapnel. Effective against hordes, whether that be against the Possessed, or an attempt at a worker's union.
### 285 x1
Spellcard 'Blaster' Type
### 286 x1
^#8FAED3;Attunes the wand to explode, dealing significant damage to anything within a massive radius.^reset;

^red;The bearer and any nearby allies are not immune to this, and will most likely die in the process.^reset;

''The Dynasty must survive. It's just a pleasant bonus if it keeps us alive.''
### 287 x1
Spellcard 'Explosion' Type
### 288 x1
^#8FAED3;Charges two lobbed shots. Attuned staff would be somewhat comparable to a double-shot incendiary launcher.^reset;
An elementary attunement powered-up to lethal levels. Whilst its older and weaker ''fireball'' cousin would remain a staple for most hardline traditionalist magicians, these lobbed fire shots have proved themselves deadly as field artillery provided the wand is charged by a suitable sorcerer. A weaker student may find their shot only reaching as far as 50 metres, unsuited for siege bombardment, but still enough for close-ranged fire suppression.
### 289 x1
Spellcard 'Lobber' Type
### 290 x1
The original AR magazine. Extensively used during the American Vietnam War due to the technical restraints of the era. The now standard 30 round mags wouldn't go on to see widespread use until the mid to late 70's, well after the end of the war. These mags still tend to pop up & are used by small colonial armies on an extreme budget & law enforcement. Only compatible with the XM177 Commando.

### 321 x1
A field battery that can rapidly recharge an ILWS magazine. ''Laser'' is an acronym for light amplification by stimulated emission of radiation. A laser beam can cut through steel while a flashlight cannot due to the fact that laser light is coherent. This means all the photons in the beam are ''in step'' with each other. By analogy, a unit of army troops marching in step can inadvertently cause a bridge to collapse, while the same number of people using the bridge in a random fashion have no effect.
  - Project Rho

### 363 x1
Basic Type III-E28 Ceramic Plating Ballistic Protection is commonly used by contemporary armies in the former and current United Systems. It grants the highest form of ballistic protection for its weight. While firearms may have reduced the overall effectiveness of armor, personal protection always remains key. Nobody wants to die, after all.

^green;+20% SMALL-ARMS, MELEE, and PHYSICAL Resistance.^reset;
^green;Prevents Medium Bleeding^reset; | ^red;-20% Movement Speed.
### 364 x1
Type III-E28 Ceramic Plate
### 365 x1
Chainmail armor of thin interlinking rings of steel fitted into a modern ballistic plate frame. Seemingly popular due to its ease of crafting (albeit time-consuming), respectable damage absorption, and light weight. Utterly useless when compared to actual purpose-built armors.

^green;+5% SMALL-ARMS Resistance.^reset;
^green;+20% MELEE and PHYSICAL Resistance.^reset;
### 366 x1
Contemporary Chain Armor Plate
### 367 x1
A form-fitting heater that can be slotted into plate carriers, with parts for a person's extremities which are likely to suffer from frostbite such as the hands and feet. Normal insulation works by trapping body heat, so if an individual gets wet from sweat or rain, or if a person stops exerting themselves, the insulation may not keep them warm. With heated garments, a person can keep warm even if they are resting and not producing heat, or if their coat is damp from sweat. ^green;Protects against Khurtovynan Frosts and Cold Weather.^reset;
### 368 x1
Heated Plate

### 1444 x1
The last project funded by the Cyborg Research Divisions on Gazri. Designed for the common infantry, this weapon retunes the standard Infantry Laser Weapons System to output a much greater damage output against vehicles on the move. Whilst not as stealthy as more portable options, it makes up for it in its 'always-ready' deployed firing state.
  
  The AUG Infantry Laser Cannon System is an alternative cheaper option to dedicated ballistic anti-tank weaponry but suffers from its unwieldy nature and high-energy use.
### 1445 x1

### 1446 x1
A literal handcannon. Most can wield these, at a heavy price in mobility. Boasting a shell diameter of 20mm firing standard tungsten AP rounds, this firearm was developed as a complement to the former Cyborg Battalions of the Gazrian Planetary Guard of the United Systems.
  
  The gun's success was short-lived due to being absolutely overkill against standard non-augmented infantry and causing common shortages of 20mm ammunition better equipped on actual vehicles for anti-aircraft weaponry. Production is few and far between but remains popular in the hands of Hunters.

### 1894 x1
The Bowler Hat first appeared in human records in 1849 by two London men as part of an order for a greater company. The design criteria was a close-fitting low-crowned hat to protect gamekeepers from low-hanging branches whilst on horseback. The keepers had previously worn top hats, which were easily knocked off. These styles of hats become popular amongst the working class in 19th Century Britain.

### 3346 x1



 Thanks to subsidies from colonial governments and certain administrators, engineers like this one 
 only require that clients bring specific parts and the host item for conversion or modification. 
 Engineered Munitions can provide effective situational benefits against a variety of targets.
 
 These services give the average adventurer an edge in particularly dangerous territories, 
 -perhaps so that they might bring something of value back to the colony. 
 
 Faction Embassies sometimes sell pre-engineered items that cannot be crafted here.
